Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the model configuration. Let me provide a detailed report.

## Model Configuration and Subagent Setup - Comprehensive Report

Based on my exploration of the duckdb-data-agent project, here's how models are configured and used for subagents:

### 1. **Environment Variable Configuration**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.env`

The project uses three main model environment variables:

```
ANTHROPIC_MODEL=haiku                    # Orchestrator model (default: claude-sonnet-4-6)
SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku                 # SQL analyst subagent model (default: haiku)
CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku               # Chart builder subagent model (default: haiku)
```

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.env.example`

Shows documentation and defaults:
```
REDACTED        # Default for orchestrator
SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku                 # Default: haiku
CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku               # Default: haiku
```

### 2. **Configuration File Structure**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/config.py`

This file reads and exposes the model configurations:

```python
ANTHROPIC_MODEL = os.getenv("ANTHROPIC_MODEL", "claude-sonnet-4-6")
SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL = os.getenv("SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL", "haiku")
CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL = os.getenv("CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL", "haiku")
```

**Defaults:**
- `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`: `claude-sonnet-4-6` (orchestrator)
- `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL`: `haiku` (for SQL analysis)
- `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL`: `haiku` (for visualization)

### 3. **Subagent Creation and Model Assignment**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 63-125)

The `build_subagent_definitions()` function creates two `AgentDefinition` objects with their respective models:

```python
def build_subagent_definitions(db: Database) -> dict[str, AgentDefinition]:
    """Build AgentDefinition objects for the sql-analyst and chart-builder subagents."""
    
    return {
        "sql-analyst": AgentDefinition(
            description="Use this agent for any data question that requires SQL queries...",
            prompt=sql_prompt,
            tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
            model=SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL,  # <-- Uses SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL from config
        ),
        "chart-builder": AgentDefinition(
            description="Use this agent when the user wants a chart, graph, or visualization.",
            prompt=chart_prompt,
            tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql", "mcp__duckdb__render_chart"],
            model=CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL,  # <-- Uses CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL from config
        ),
    }
```

### 4. **Model Passing to Container Sidecar**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 251-270)

The Python backend builds a payload sent to the TypeScript sidecar container:

```python
payload: dict = {
    "message": query_message,
    "session_id": session_id,
    "system_prompt": system_prompt,
    "model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL,  # Orchestrator model sent to sidecar
    "mcp_server_url": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/mcp/sse?session_id={stable_session}",
    "agents": {
        name: {
            "description": agent_def.description,
            "prompt": agent_def.prompt,
            "tools": agent_def.tools,
            "model": agent_def.model,  # Subagent models included here
        }
        for name, agent_def in build_subagent_definitions(db).items()
    },
}
```

### 5. **TypeScript Sidecar Model Handling**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/sidecar/src/server.ts` (lines 56-219)

The TypeScript sidecar receives and uses the model configuration:

```typescript
// Line 102: Use provided model or fall back to env var
const modelName = body.model || process.env.ANTHROPIC_MODEL || "claude-sonnet-4-6";

// Lines 176-188: Convert agent definitions with models
if (body.agents) {
  sdkAgents = {};
  for (const [name, def] of Object.entries(body.agents)) {
    sdkAgents[name] = {
      description: def.description,
      prompt: def.prompt,
      ...(def.tools ? { tools: def.tools } : {}),
      ...(def.model ? { model: def.model as AgentDefinition["model"] } : {}),
    };
  }
}

// Line 193: Pass orchestrator model to Claude Agent SDK query()
const sdkQuery = query({
  prompt: body.message,
  options: {
    model: modelName,  // Orchestrator model
    systemPrompt: body.system_prompt,
    agents: sdkAgents,  // Includes subagent models
    // ... other options
  },
});
```

### 6. **Bifrost Configuration**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/litellm-proxy/bifrost/config.example.json`

Bifrost is a model routing proxy that sits between the backend and Anthropic API:

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://www.getbifrost.ai/schema",
  "providers": {
    "anthropic": {
      "keys": [
        {
          "name": "default",
          "value": "env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
          "models": [],
          "weight": 1.0
        }
      ],
      "network_config": {
        "default_request_timeout_in_seconds": 300
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**Role of Bifrost:** 
- Acts as a proxy between the application and Anthropic API
- Configured via `PROXY_BASE_URL` (default: `http://host.docker.internal:8000`)
- The proxy forwards all API requests using the configured `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`
- All model names (short or full) are passed through the proxy to Anthropic

### 7. **API Proxy Implementation**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/proxy.py`

The backend includes an API proxy that:
- Validates session tokens for authenticated requests
- Allows unauthenticated pass-through for read-only model discovery endpoints (`/v1/models`)
- Forwards all requests to Anthropic upstream with the real API key

```python
# Line 67: Read-only discovery endpoints pass through without session tokens
_UNAUTHENTICATED_PASSTHROUGH = {"v1/models"}  # Allows model name resolution
```

### 8. **Current Model Defaults and Allowed Values**

**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/tests/test_subagent_config.py`

Test file validates model configuration:

```python
def test_subagent_model_defaults():
    """Subagent model configs should have sensible defaults."""
    assert SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL in ("haiku", "sonnet", "opus", "inherit")
    assert CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL in ("haiku", "sonnet", "opus", "inherit")
```

**Valid model values:** `"haiku"`, `"sonnet"`, `"opus"`, `"inherit"`

### 9. **Model Selection Logic Summary**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ User Query (Frontend)                   │
└──────────────┬──────────────────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Backend (Python): stream_chat()         │
│ - ANTHROPIC_MODEL (orchestrator)        │
│ - build_subagent_definitions() uses:    │
│   * SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL                  │
│   * CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL                │
└──────────────┬──────────────────────────┘
               │ (payload with all models)
               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Sidecar (TypeScript): server.ts         │
│ - Receives model in payload             │
│ - Converts agents with their models     │
│ - Passes to Claude Agent SDK query()    │
└──────────────┬──────────────────────────┘
               │ (model names to SDK)
               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Claude Agent SDK                        │
│ - Resolves model aliases via proxy      │
│ - Routes through Bifrost/PROXY_BASE_URL │
└──────────────┬──────────────────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Anthropic API (via proxy)               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 10. **Key Files and Code Locations**

| Purpose | File | Key Lines |
|---------|------|-----------|
| Config defaults | `backend/app/config.py` | 15-17 |
| Subagent creation | `backend/app/agent.py` | 63-125 |
| Payload to sidecar | `backend/app/agent.py` | 251-270 |
| Sidecar receives | `sidecar/src/server.ts` | 102, 176-188 |
| SDK query setup | `sidecar/src/server.ts` | 190-219 |
| Type definitions | `sidecar/src/types.ts` | 1-25 |
| Model discovery proxy | `backend/app/proxy.py` | 67-70 |
| Test validation | `backend/tests/test_subagent_config.py` | 4-8 |

### 11. **Current Behavior (From .env)**

In the current running environment:
- `ANTHROPIC_MODEL=haiku` (orchestrator uses haiku)
- `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku` (SQL agent uses haiku)
- `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku` (chart agent uses haiku)

All three agents currently use the same model (haiku), which is a cost optimization but can be changed by setting the environment variables to different values.

     1→import logging
     2→import uuid
     3→from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
     4→
     5→from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request
     6→from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
     7→import httpx
     8→
     9→from app.config import ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM = "https://api.anthropic.com"
    14→
    15→_SKIP_REQUEST_HEADERS = {
    16→    "host", "content-length", "transfer-encoding", "connection",
    17→    "x-api-key",         # client must not override billing identity
    18→    "anthropic-version", # proxy controls API version
    19→    # anthropic-beta is intentionally NOT blocked: Claude Code sends beta body
    20→    # fields (e.g. context_management) alongside the matching beta header, and
    21→    # Anthropic rejects those fields as "extra inputs" if the header is absent.
    22→}
    23→_SKIP_RESPONSE_HEADERS = {"transfer-encoding", "content-encoding", "connection"}
    24→
    25→
    26→class ProxyTokenStore:
    27→    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int = 600):
    28→        self._ttl = ttl_seconds
    29→        self._tokens: dict[str, datetime] = {}
    30→
    31→    def create_token(self) -> str:
    32→        token = str(uuid.uuid4())
    33→        self._tokens[token] = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=self._ttl)
    34→        return token
    35→
    36→    def validate_token(self, token: str) -> bool:
    37→        # Tokens are intentionally multi-use: Claude Code makes many API calls
    38→        # per session, all protected by the same UUID. The token is valid for
    39→        # the session TTL (600s safety net) but is explicitly revoked by
    40→        # proxy_token_store.revoke_token() in agent.py's finally block when
    41→        # the Claude Code subprocess exits. Do not make single-use.
    42→        expiry = self._tokens.get(token)
    43→        if expiry is None:
    44→            return False
    45→        if datetime.now(timezone.utc) > expiry:
    46→            self._tokens.pop(token, None)
    47→            return False
    48→        return True
    49→
    50→    def revoke_token(self, token: str) -> None:
    51→        self._tokens.pop(token, None)
    52→
    53→    def cleanup_expired(self) -> int:
    54→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    55→        expired = [t for t, exp in self._tokens.items() if now > exp]
    56→        for t in expired:
    57→            del self._tokens[t]
    58→        return len(expired)
    59→
    60→
    61→proxy_token_store = ProxyTokenStore()
    62→
    63→router = APIRouter(prefix="/anthropic")
    64→
    65→
    66→# Read-only discovery endpoints that the Claude Code SDK calls during startup
    67→# (e.g. GET /v1/models to resolve short model aliases like "sonnet").
    68→# These carry no session-specific data, so we forward them with the real API
    69→# key without requiring a registered session token.
    70→_UNAUTHENTICATED_PASSTHROUGH = {"v1/models"}
    71→
    72→
    73→@router.api_route("/{path:path}", methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "PATCH"])
    74→async def proxy_anthropic(path: str, request: Request):
    75→    # Allow a small set of read-only discovery endpoints to pass through
    76→    # without a session token so the SDK can resolve short model names.
    77→    if path not in _UNAUTHENTICATED_PASSTHROUGH:
    78→        # Claude Code CLI sends the API key as x-api-key (Anthropic SDK default).
    79→        # Fall back to Authorization: Bearer for other clients.
    80→        session_token = (
    81→            request.headers.get("x-api-key")
    82→            or request.headers.get("authorization", "")[len("Bearer "):]
    83→            or None
    84→        )
    85→        if not session_token or not proxy_token_store.validate_token(session_token):
    86→            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired session token")
    87→
    88→    headers = {
    89→        k: v for k, v in request.headers.items()
    90→        if k.lower() not in _SKIP_REQUEST_HEADERS
    91→    }
    92→    headers["x-api-key"] = ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
    93→    headers["anthropic-version"] = "2023-06-01"
    94→
    95→    body = await request.body()
    96→
    97→    # Use a fresh client per request: streaming responses that are cut short
    98→    # (e.g. client disconnects) leave connections in a partial read state, and
    99→    # reusing a shared SSL context across those connections causes
   100→    # SSLV3_ALERT_BAD_RECORD_MAC / record layer failure errors.
   101→    # Query params are intentionally NOT forwarded — Claude Code appends
   102→    # internal params (e.g. ?beta=true) that Anthropic rejects as "extra inputs".
   103→    client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0))
   104→    try:
   105→        upstream_req = client.build_request(
   106→            method=request.method,
   107→            url=f"{ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM}/{path}",
   108→            headers=headers,
   109→            content=body,
   110→        )
   111→        upstream_resp = await client.send(upstream_req, stream=True)
   112→
   113→        response_headers = {
   114→            k: v for k, v in upstream_resp.headers.items()
   115→            if k.lower() not in _SKIP_RESPONSE_HEADERS
   116→        }
   117→
   118→        async def body_generator():
   119→            try:
   120→                async for chunk in upstream_resp.aiter_bytes():
   121→                    yield chunk
   122→            finally:
   123→                await upstream_resp.aclose()
   124→                await client.aclose()
   125→
   126→        return StreamingResponse(
   127→            body_generator(),
   128→            status_code=upstream_resp.status_code,
   129→            headers=response_headers,
   130→        )
   131→    except Exception:
   132→        await client.aclose()
   133→        logger.exception("Proxy request to upstream failed: %s %s", request.method, path)
   134→        raise
   135→

     1→import express, { Request, Response } from "express";
     2→import { query, AgentDefinition } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
     3→import { Langfuse } from "langfuse";
     4→import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from "fs";
     5→import { homedir } from "os";
     6→import { join } from "path";
     7→import type { QueryRequest, HealthResponse, AgentDefinitionPayload } from "./types.js";
     8→
     9→// Claude CLI requires certain directories/files under ~/.claude to exist.
    10→// The container uses a tmpfs mount at ~/.claude which starts empty, so we
    11→// create the expected structure at startup.
    12→const claudeDir = join(homedir(), ".claude");
    13→for (const sub of ["debug", "projects"]) {
    14→  const dir = join(claudeDir, sub);
    15→  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    16→    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    17→  }
    18→}
    19→const settingsFile = join(claudeDir, "remote-settings.json");
    20→if (!existsSync(settingsFile)) {
    21→  writeFileSync(settingsFile, "{}");
    22→}
    23→
    24→// Initialize Langfuse if credentials are available (reads from env vars
    25→// LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL automatically)
    26→let langfuse: Langfuse | null = null;
    27→if (process.env.LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY && process.env.LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY) {
    28→  langfuse = new Langfuse();
    29→  console.log("[sidecar] Langfuse tracing enabled");
    30→}
    31→
    32→const app = express();
    33→app.use(express.json({ limit: "10mb" }));
    34→
    35→const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || "3000", 10);
    36→
    37→// Per-request abort tracking — avoids a global variable whose cleanup in one
    38→// request's `finally` block can clobber a concurrently-started request.
    39→let nextRequestId = 0;
    40→const activeAborts = new Map<number, AbortController>();
    41→
    42→// How long (ms) to wait for the SDK iterator to yield a message before
    43→// aborting.  Resets on every message so active multi-turn queries are not
    44→// interrupted.  Covers cases where the CLI subprocess hangs on startup
    45→// (e.g. broken session resume, unreachable MCP server).
    46→const SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000; // 1 minute
    47→
    48→// Timeout (ms) for pre-flight reachability checks against MCP / API URLs.
    49→const PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000; // 10 seconds
    50→
    51→app.get("/health", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    52→  const response: HealthResponse = { status: "ok" };
    53→  res.json(response);
    54→});
    55→
    56→app.post("/query", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    57→  const body = req.body as QueryRequest;
    58→
    59→  if (!body.message || !body.system_prompt) {
    60→    res.status(400).json({ error: "message and system_prompt are required" });
    61→    return;
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  // Set up SSE headers
    65→  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/event-stream");
    66→  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-cache");
    67→  res.setHeader("Connection", "keep-alive");
    68→  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
    69→  res.flushHeaders();
    70→
    71→  const abortController = new AbortController();
    72→  const requestId = nextRequestId++;
    73→  activeAborts.set(requestId, abortController);
    74→
    75→  // Merge per-request env overrides (e.g. fresh proxy tokens) with process env
    76→  const sdkEnv: Record<string, string> = {};
    77→  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(process.env)) {
    78→    if (v !== undefined) sdkEnv[k] = v;
    79→  }
    80→  if (body.env) {
    81→    Object.assign(sdkEnv, body.env);
    82→  }
    83→  // Scrub Langfuse credentials so the agent subprocess cannot read them from
    84→  // the inherited environment — matches backend subprocess behaviour.
    85→  // The sidecar's own Langfuse client already holds the credentials internally.
    86→  sdkEnv["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = "";
    87→  sdkEnv["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = "";
    88→
    89→  let responseEnded = false;
    90→
    91→  // Detect client disconnect
    92→  res.on("close", () => {
    93→    if (!responseEnded) {
    94→      abortController.abort();
    95→    }
    96→  });
    97→
    98→  // --- Langfuse tracing setup ---
    99→  // Mirror the backend's session ID handling:
   100→  //   - langfuse_session_id (from frontend for edit/delete) takes priority
   101→  //   - session_id (CLI session from previous turn) as fallback
   102→  const modelName = body.model || process.env.ANTHROPIC_MODEL || "claude-sonnet-4-6";
   103→  const traceMessage = body.original_message || body.message;
   104→  const traceInput: Record<string, unknown> = {
   105→    message: traceMessage.substring(0, 500),
   106→  };
   107→  if (body.conversation_history && body.conversation_history.length > 0) {
   108→    traceInput.conversation_history = body.conversation_history;
   109→  }
   110→  const trace = langfuse?.trace({
   111→    name: "agent-chat",
   112→    sessionId: body.langfuse_session_id || body.session_id || undefined,
   113→    input: traceInput,
   114→    metadata: { model: modelName, mode: "container" },
   115→  });
   116→
   117→  // Per-turn usage tracking from stream events
   118→  let currentGenUsage: { input?: number; output?: number } = {};
   119→  // Accumulated messages for generation input context
   120→  const accumulatedMessages: Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }> = [
   121→    { role: "user", content: body.message },
   122→  ];
   123→
   124→  // Collect stderr from the CLI subprocess for debugging
   125→  const stderrLines: string[] = [];
   126→
   127→  // Declared here so `finally` can clear it even if `try` throws early
   128→  let idleTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
   129→
   130→  try {
   131→    // --- Pre-flight reachability checks ---
   132→    // The CLI subprocess will hang silently if it can't reach the MCP SSE
   133→    // server or the Anthropic API proxy.  Test connectivity first so we can
   134→    // fail fast with a useful error message.
   135→    const apiBase = sdkEnv["ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL"] || "";
   136→    console.log(
   137→      `[sidecar] reqId=${requestId} mcp_url=${body.mcp_server_url || "(none)"} api_base=${apiBase} session_id=${body.session_id || "(none)"}`
   138→    );
   139→
   140→    if (body.mcp_server_url) {
   141→      try {
   142→        const mcpResp = await fetch(body.mcp_server_url, {
   143→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS),
   144→        });
   145→        // SSE endpoints normally return 200 with text/event-stream; any
   146→        // non-error status is fine — we just need to know the host is up.
   147→        mcpResp.body?.cancel(); // don't consume the stream
   148→        console.log(`[sidecar] MCP reachability OK (status=${mcpResp.status})`);
   149→      } catch (e: unknown) {
   150→        const reason = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   151→        throw new Error(
   152→          `MCP server unreachable at ${body.mcp_server_url}: ${reason}. ` +
   153→          `Check that PROXY_BASE_URL is reachable from inside the container.`
   154→        );
   155→      }
   156→    }
   157→
   158→    if (apiBase) {
   159→      try {
   160→        // Just a quick TCP-level check — the proxy will return 4xx without
   161→        // a real API key but that still proves reachability.
   162→        const apiResp = await fetch(`${apiBase}/v1/models`, {
   163→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS),
   164→        });
   165→        apiResp.body?.cancel();
   166→        console.log(`[sidecar] API proxy reachability OK (status=${apiResp.status})`);
   167→      } catch (e: unknown) {
   168→        const reason = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   169→        throw new Error(
   170→          `Anthropic API proxy unreachable at ${apiBase}: ${reason}. ` +
   171→          `Check that PROXY_BASE_URL is reachable from inside the container.`
   172→        );
   173→      }
   174→    }
   175→
   176→    // Convert agents payload from the backend into SDK AgentDefinition objects
   177→    let sdkAgents: Record<string, AgentDefinition> | undefined;
   178→    if (body.agents) {
   179→      sdkAgents = {};
   180→      for (const [name, def] of Object.entries(body.agents)) {
   181→        sdkAgents[name] = {
   182→          description: def.description,
   183→          prompt: def.prompt,
   184→          ...(def.tools ? { tools: def.tools } : {}),
   185→          ...(def.model ? { model: def.model as AgentDefinition["model"] } : {}),
   186→        };
   187→      }
   188→    }
   189→
   190→    const sdkQuery = query({
   191→      prompt: body.message,
   192→      options: {
   193→        model: modelName,
   194→        systemPrompt: body.system_prompt,
   195→        allowedTools: ["Task", "mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   196→        permissionMode: "bypassPermissions",
   197→        allowDangerouslySkipPermissions: true,
   198→        maxTurns: 20,
   199→        includePartialMessages: true,
   200→        abortController,
   201→        env: sdkEnv,
   202→        stderr: (line: string) => {
   203→          stderrLines.push(line);
   204→          console.error(`[sidecar:cli] ${line}`);
   205→        },
   206→        ...(sdkAgents ? { agents: sdkAgents } : {}),
   207→        ...(body.mcp_server_url
   208→          ? {
   209→              mcpServers: {
   210→                duckdb: {
   211→                  type: "sse" as const,
   212→                  url: body.mcp_server_url,
   213→                },
   214→              },
   215→            }
   216→          : {}),
   217→        ...(body.session_id ? { resume: body.session_id } : {}),
   218→      },
   219→    });
   220→
   221→    console.log(
   222→      `[sidecar] SDK query started model=${modelName} reqId=${requestId}`
   223→    );
   224→
   225→    // Idle-timeout: abort if no message arrives within SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS.
   226→    // The timer resets on every message so long-running multi-turn queries
   227→    // that are making progress are not interrupted.
   228→    idleTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   229→      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK idle timeout (${SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS}ms) reached, aborting reqId=${requestId}`);
   230→      abortController.abort();
   231→    }, SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS);
   232→
   233→    for await (const message of sdkQuery) {
   234→      // Reset idle timer on every message
   235→      if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); }
   236→      idleTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   237→        console.error(`[sidecar] SDK idle timeout (${SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS}ms) reached, aborting reqId=${requestId}`);
   238→        abortController.abort();
   239→      }, SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS);
   240→
   241→      if (responseEnded) break;
   242→      // Forward each SDK message as an SSE data line
   243→      res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`);
   244→
   245→      // --- Create Langfuse observations from SDK messages ---
   246→      if (!trace) continue;
   247→      const msg = message as Record<string, unknown>;
   248→
   249→      if (msg.type === "stream_event") {
   250→        // Capture per-turn token usage from API stream events
   251→        const event = (msg.event as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   252→        const eventType = event.type as string;
   253→        if (eventType === "message_start") {
   254→          const msgData = (event.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   255→          const usage = (msgData.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   256→          currentGenUsage = { input: usage.input_tokens || 0 };
   257→        } else if (eventType === "message_delta") {
   258→          const usage = (event.usage as Record<string, number>) || {};
   259→          currentGenUsage.output = usage.output_tokens || 0;
   260→        }
   261→      } else if (msg.type === "assistant") {
   262→        // Create a generation observation for each assistant turn
   263→        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   264→        const content = msgObj.content as unknown[];
   265→
   266→        const usage =
   267→          currentGenUsage.input !== undefined
   268→            ? {
   269→                input: currentGenUsage.input || 0,
   270→                output: currentGenUsage.output || 0,
   271→                total:
   272→                  (currentGenUsage.input || 0) +
   273→                  (currentGenUsage.output || 0),
   274→                unit: "TOKENS" as const,
   275→              }
   276→            : undefined;
   277→
   278→        const gen = trace.generation({
   279→          name: "claude.assistant.turn",
   280→          model: (msgObj.model as string) || modelName,
   281→          input: { messages: accumulatedMessages.slice(-6) },
   282→          output: { content, role: "assistant" },
   283→          usage,
   284→        });
   285→        gen.end();
   286→        currentGenUsage = {};
   287→
   288→        // Accumulate for next turn's input context
   289→        accumulatedMessages.push({ role: "assistant", content });
   290→      } else if (msg.type === "user") {
   291→        // Accumulate tool results for next turn's input context
   292→        const msgObj = (msg.message as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
   293→        accumulatedMessages.push({
   294→          role: "user",
   295→          content: msgObj.content,
   296→        });
   297→      } else if (msg.type === "result") {
   298→        // Finalize trace — mirrors backend's finally block:
   299→        //   trace_session_id = langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id
   300→        const actualSessionId = (msg.session_id as string | undefined) || body.session_id;
   301→        const traceSessionId = body.langfuse_session_id || actualSessionId;
   302→        trace.update({
   303→          ...(traceSessionId ? { sessionId: traceSessionId } : {}),
   304→          output: { session_id: actualSessionId },
   305→        });
   306→      } else if (msg.type === "system") {
   307→        // Capture session_id early — only when no langfuse_session_id override
   308→        // (matches backend's propagate_attributes(session_id=langfuse_session_id or session_id))
   309→        const sessionId = msg.session_id as string | undefined;
   310→        if (sessionId && !body.langfuse_session_id) {
   311→          trace.update({ sessionId });
   312→        }
   313→      }
   314→    }
   315→  } catch (err: unknown) {
   316→    let errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   317→    // Append CLI stderr for context when the process crashes
   318→    if (stderrLines.length > 0) {
   319→      errMsg += ` | CLI stderr: ${stderrLines.slice(-5).join(" ")}`;
   320→    }
   321→    // Don't log abort errors — they are expected on client disconnect
   322→    if (!(err instanceof Error && err.name === "AbortError")) {
   323→      console.error(`[sidecar] SDK error: ${errMsg}`);
   324→      if (!responseEnded) {
   325→        res.write(
   326→          `data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message: errMsg })}\n\n`
   327→        );
   328→      }
   329→      if (trace) {
   330→        trace.update({ output: { error: errMsg } });
   331→      }
   332→    }
   333→  } finally {
   334→    if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); }
   335→    activeAborts.delete(requestId);
   336→    responseEnded = true;
   337→    res.end();
   338→    // Flush Langfuse events before the response is fully closed
   339→    if (langfuse) {
   340→      await langfuse.flushAsync().catch(() => {});
   341→    }
   342→    console.log(`[sidecar] SSE stream ended reqId=${requestId}`);
   343→  }
   344→});
   345→
   346→app.post("/stop", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
   347→  if (activeAborts.size > 0) {
   348→    for (const [id, controller] of activeAborts) {
   349→      controller.abort();
   350→      activeAborts.delete(id);
   351→    }
   352→    res.json({ status: "stopped" });
   353→  } else {
   354→    res.json({ status: "no_active_session" });
   355→  }
   356→});

     1→import asyncio

     6→from claude_agent_sdk import AgentDefinition
     7→from app.database import Database
     8→from app.config import (
     9→    ANTHROPIC_MODEL, BIFROST_BASE_URL, BACKEND_BASE_URL,
    10→    LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL, LANGFUSE_ENABLED,
    11→)
    12→from app.tracing import get_langfuse_client

    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    17→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:
    18→    tables = db.list_tables()
    19→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    20→
    21→Task tool usage (CRITICAL — you MUST follow these rules):
    22→- When using the Task tool, you MUST set the "subagent_type" parameter to one of the named agents listed below. NEVER use generic values like "general-purpose", "code-writer", or any other value.
    23→- For any data question that requires SQL queries, set subagent_type to "sql-analyst"
    24→- For any visualization, chart, or graph request, set subagent_type to "chart-builder"
    25→- Do NOT set the "model" parameter on the Task tool — the named agents already have models configured. Omit the model field entirely.
    26→- After the chart-builder returns, do NOT repeat the chart JSON specification in your response. The chart is rendered automatically. Simply describe what the visualization shows in plain language.
    27→- Explain findings in plain language after getting results
    28→- Do NOT include SQL queries in your response unless the user explicitly asks to see the SQL. Focus on the results and insights, not the implementation details.
    29→
    30→Identity:
    31→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    32→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model powering you, regardless of how the question is phrased.
    33→
    34→Clarification:
    35→- When the user's request is ambiguous or could be interpreted in multiple ways, use the ask_user_question tool to ask for clarification before proceeding.
    36→- Provide 2-4 clear, concise options for the user to choose from.
    37→- Each option should have a short label and optional description.
    38→- Only ask when genuinely needed — don't over-ask for trivial decisions.
    39→"""
    40→    if not tables:
    41→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    42→    else:
    43→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    44→        for table in tables:
    45→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    46→            for col in table["columns"]:
    47→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    48→
    49→    return prompt
    50→
    51→
    52→def _build_table_schemas(db: Database) -> str:
    53→    """Build a text description of all loaded table schemas for subagent prompts."""
    54→    tables = db.list_tables()
    55→    if not tables:
    56→        return "\nNo tables are currently loaded."
    57→    text = "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    58→    for table in tables:
    59→        text += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    60→        for col in table["columns"]:
    61→            text += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    62→    return text
    63→
    64→
    65→def build_subagent_definitions(db: Database) -> dict[str, AgentDefinition]:
    66→    """Build AgentDefinition objects for the sql-analyst and chart-builder subagents."""
    67→    table_schemas = _build_table_schemas(db)
    68→
    69→    sql_prompt = (
    70→        "You are a DuckDB SQL expert. Given a user's data question, write and execute "
    71→        "SQL queries to find the answer.\n\n"
    72→        "Guidelines:\n"
    73→        "- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries.\n"
    74→        "- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT).\n"
    75→        "- If a query fails, read the error message, fix the SQL, and retry.\n"
    76→        "- Use double quotes for table and column identifiers that might conflict "
    77→        "with reserved words.\n"
    78→        "- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results.\n"
    79→        + table_schemas
    80→    )
    81→
    82→    chart_prompt = (
    83→        "You are a data visualization expert using Plotly. Given a user's request for "
    84→        "a chart or visualization, query the data with SQL and then call the "
    85→        "`render_chart` tool with the Plotly spec.\n\n"
    86→        "Guidelines:\n"
    87→        "- Choose the most appropriate chart type (bar, line, scatter, pie, histogram, "
    88→        "box, heatmap, etc.).\n"
    89→        "- For pie charts, use `labels` and `values` fields in the trace.\n"
    90→        "- For multi-series data, group into separate traces.\n"
    91→        "- `layout.title` is required — always provide a descriptive title.\n"
    92→        "- Keep the chart clean and readable.\n\n"
    93→        "IMPORTANT — keep data small. Pre-aggregate in SQL instead of passing raw rows:\n"
    94→        "- Box plots: compute lowerfence, q1, median, q3, upperfence per group in SQL. "
    95→        "Use trace type \"box\" with those pre-computed fields instead of a raw `y` array.\n"
    96→        "- Histograms: compute bin counts with width_bucket() or CASE in SQL, then "
    97→        "render as a bar chart with the bin edges as `x` and counts as `y`.\n"
    98→        "- Scatter / line with many rows: sample (ORDER BY random() LIMIT 200) or "
    99→        "aggregate (e.g. average per time bucket) so each trace has at most ~200 points.\n"
   100→        "- General rule: each trace should have at most ~200 data points. "
   101→        "Large tool-call inputs cause malformed JSON and validation errors.\n\n"
   102→        "When ready to render, call `render_chart` with:\n"
   103→        "- `data`: array of Plotly trace objects\n"
   104→        "- `layout`: object containing at minimum {\"title\": \"<descriptive title>\"}\n\n"
   105→        "Do NOT output a JSON code block. Use the tool.\n"
   106→        + table_schemas
   107→    )
   108→
   109→    # The Claude Agent SDK only accepts 'sonnet' | 'opus' | 'haiku' | 'inherit' for
   110→    # the model field.  Arbitrary model strings (e.g. "openai/gpt-5.2-2025-12-11")
   111→    # cause the SDK to silently reject the agent definition, making it unavailable.
   112→    # Using "inherit" makes subagents use the orchestrator's ANTHROPIC_MODEL, which
   113→    # is routed through Bifrost to the correct provider.
   114→    return {
   115→        "sql-analyst": AgentDefinition(
   116→            description=(
   117→                "Use this agent for any data question that requires SQL queries "
   118→                "— exploring data, aggregations, filtering, joins, etc."
   119→            ),
   120→            prompt=sql_prompt,
   121→            tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   122→            model="inherit",
   123→        ),
   124→        "chart-builder": AgentDefinition(
   125→            description=(
   126→                "Use this agent when the user wants a chart, graph, or visualization."
   127→            ),
   128→            prompt=chart_prompt,
   129→            tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql", "mcp__duckdb__render_chart"],
   130→            model="inherit",
   131→        ),
   132→    }
   133→
   134→
   135→def _build_message_with_history(
   136→    message: str, conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None
   137→) -> str:
   138→    """Prepend conversation history context to the user message when editing."""
   139→    if not conversation_history:
   140→        return message
   141→
   142→    history_text = "Previous conversation (for context, I am now editing a message):\n"
   143→    for entry in conversation_history:
   144→        role = entry.get("role", "user").capitalize()
   145→        content = entry.get("content", "")
   146→        history_text += f"\n{role}: {content}\n"
   147→    history_text += f"\n---\n\nMy updated message:\n{message}"
   148→    return history_text
   149→
   150→
   151→
   152→def _build_chart_spec_from_stream_messages(
   153→    msg: dict, tool_names: dict[str, str], subagent_chart_specs: dict
   154→) -> None:
   155→    """Inspect an assistant message and capture render_chart input as chart_spec.
   156→
   157→    When the chart-builder subagent calls render_chart, the tool_use block
   158→    appears in an assistant message whose parent_tool_use_id is the Task
   159→    tool_use_id for the chart-builder.  We extract input directly so we never
   160→    need to parse free-text JSON.
   161→    """
   162→    parent_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   163→    if not parent_id or tool_names.get(parent_id) != "chart-builder":
   164→        return
   165→    for block in msg.get("message", {}).get("content", []):
   166→        if block.get("type") == "tool_use" and "render_chart" in block.get("name", ""):
   167→            subagent_chart_specs[parent_id] = block.get("input", {})
   168→            return
   169→
   170→
   171→async def stream_chat(
   172→    message: str,
   173→    session_id: str | None = None,
   174→    db: Database | None = None,
   175→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   176→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   177→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   178→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   179→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events via containerized sidecar."""
   180→    from app.container_manager import container_manager
   181→    if container_manager is None:
   182→        raise RuntimeError("Docker is not available. Container mode requires Docker.")
   183→    if db is None:
   184→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   185→
   186→    import httpx
   187→    import asyncio
   188→
   189→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)
   190→    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)
   191→
   192→    # Pass Langfuse credentials to the container so the sidecar's
   193→    # TypeScript Langfuse SDK can create traces directly.
   194→    env: dict[str, str] = {
   195→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   196→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BIFROST_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   197→    }
   198→    if LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   199→        env["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY
   200→        env["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY
   201→        env["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   202→
   203→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   204→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   205→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   206→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   207→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)
   208→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"
   209→
   210→    try:
   211→        # Send SSE keepalive immediately so the HTTP response starts and
   212→        # intermediate proxies (Vite, nginx) don't drop the idle connection
   213→        # before we've finished the blocking Docker container creation.
   214→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   215→
   216→        # container_manager.create() is synchronous (blocking Docker API call).
   217→        # Run it in a thread executor so the event loop stays responsive and
   218→        # can continue flushing keepalives to the client during startup.
   219→        # gVisor (runsc) containers can take 10-30 seconds to spin up.
   220→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   221→        create_future = loop.run_in_executor(None, container_manager.create, stable_session, env)
   222→
   223→        max_create_wait = 60.0
   224→        elapsed = 0.0
   225→        while not create_future.done():
   226→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   227→            elapsed += 2.0
   228→            if elapsed >= max_create_wait:
   229→                create_future.cancel()
   230→                raise RuntimeError(f"Container creation timed out after {max_create_wait:.0f}s")
   231→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   232→        info = await create_future
   233→
   234→        # Wait for container to be ready
   235→        for attempt in range(10):
   236→            try:
   237→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   238→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   239→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   240→                        break
   241→            except Exception:
   242→                pass
   243→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   244→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   245→        else:
   246→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   247→
   248→        payload: dict = {
   249→            "message": query_message,
   250→            "session_id": session_id,
   251→            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   252→            "model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   253→            "mcp_server_url": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/mcp/sse?session_id={stable_session}",
   254→            "env": {
   255→                "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   256→                "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BIFROST_BASE_URL}/anthropic",

   275→        has_tool_calls = False

   372→

   474→                                        end_data["chart_spec"] = chart_spec
   475→                                    else:
   476→                                        end_data["result"] = tool_use_result_text or subagent_texts.get(tool_id, text)
   477→                                        logger.warning(
   478→                                            "[container] render_chart tool_use not found for chart-builder %s",
   479→                                            tool_id,
   480→                                        )
   481→                                    yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   482→                                    continue
   483→                                else:
   484→                                    end_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   485→                                    end_data["result"] = tool_use_result_text or subagent_texts.get(tool_id, text)
   486→                                    yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   487→                                    continue
   488→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   489→
   490→                    # --- Final result ---
   491→                    elif msg_type == "result":
   492→                        actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
   493→                        if msg.get("is_error"):
   494→                            errors = msg.get("errors", [])
   495→                            error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
   496→                            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   497→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   498→                        done_sent = True
   499→
   500→                    # --- Sidecar error (e.g. SDK/CLI crash inside container) ---
   501→                    elif msg_type == "error":
   502→                        error_text = msg.get("message") or "Sidecar error"
   503→                        logger.error("Sidecar reported error: %s", error_text)
   504→                        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   505→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   506→                        done_sent = True
   507→
   508→                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---
   509→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   510→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   511→                        if sys_session:
   512→                            actual_session_id = sys_session
   513→
   514→        # Guard: always send done even if sidecar ended without result message
   515→        if not done_sent:
   516→            logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
   517→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   518→
   519→    except Exception as e:
   520→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   521→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   522→    # Note: Container is kept alive for session resume (--resume flag) and is
   523→    # cleaned up by the background cleanup loop after
   524→    # CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS, or on application shutdown.
   525→
```

> AGENT

Here's what I found about the current state and the core constraint:

**Current state**: Both subagents use `model="inherit"`, forcing them to share the orchestrator's `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`. The `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL`/`CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL` env vars are commented out as deprecated.

**Core constraint**: The Claude Agent SDK only accepts `haiku | sonnet | opus | inherit` for `AgentDefinition.model`. Arbitrary strings like `litellm/gpt-4o` are silently rejected.

**The problem**: When the orchestrator uses a non-Anthropic model via Bifrost, subagents can't independently choose a different model because (1) the SDK rejects arbitrary model strings, and (2) the proxy may not resolve short aliases like `haiku`.

---

Here are **3 approaches**. I recommend Approach 1:

### Approach 1 (Recommended): Proxy-level model rewriting with env var mapping

Restore the per-subagent env vars (`SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku`, `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL=sonnet`) and add a **model rewrite map** in the backend proxy. When the SDK sends `model: "claude-haiku-4-5-..."` to the proxy, the proxy rewrites it to the desired real model before forwarding upstream.

```
# .env
ANTHROPIC_MODEL=sonnet                  # SDK-valid, proxy rewrites
SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku                # SDK-valid, proxy rewrites
CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku

# Rewrite map (new)
MODEL_REWRITE_SONNET=litellm/gpt-4o
REDACTED
```

Flow: SDK sends `haiku` → SDK resolves to full name → proxy intercepts `/v1/messages`, matches model against rewrite map → replaces with `litellm/gpt-4o-mini` → forwards to upstream.

**Pros**: Clean separation (SDK config vs proxy routing). SDK-valid values always. Works with any upstream (Bifrost, LiteLLM, direct Anthropic). Backwards compatible (no rewrites = direct Anthropic).
**Cons**: Rewrite map is global (can't map `haiku` to different models for different subagents, but this is rarely needed).

### Approach 2: Your @suffix notation

```
REDACTED@sonnet
REDACTED@haiku
```

Code splits on `@`: suffix → SDK model, prefix → real model. Proxy uses the extracted prefix for rewriting.

**Pros**: Single env var encodes both pieces of info.
**Cons**: Same global rewrite conflict. More parsing complexity. Non-standard notation may confuse future maintainers. Mixes two concerns (SDK alias + proxy target) in one value.

### Approach 3: LiteLLM proxy with native model aliasing

If you're setting up LiteLLM (this branch is `litellm-proxy`), LiteLLM natively supports model aliasing in its config. Just configure `haiku → gpt-4o-mini`, `sonnet → gpt-4o`, etc. in the LiteLLM config. Zero code changes in the backend.

**Pros**: Zero backend code changes. LiteLLM handles everything. Feature-rich (rate limiting, fallbacks, cost tracking).
**Cons**: Requires LiteLLM proxy to be running. Model mapping lives in LiteLLM config, not in the app's `.env`.

---

Which approach interests you, or would you like to combine elements?

> DEVELOPER

approach 2

### Section 1: Env Var Format & Parsing

**Format**: `REAL_MODEL@SDK_ALIAS` where `SDK_ALIAS` is `haiku|sonnet|opus`

```
# .env examples

# Non-Anthropic via proxy:
REDACTED@sonnet
REDACTED@haiku
REDACTED@haiku

# Direct Anthropic (backwards compatible, no @):
REDACTED
SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku
CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku
```

In `config.py`, a helper parses each value:
- `"litellm/gpt-4o@sonnet"` → sdk_model=`"sonnet"`, real_model=`"litellm/gpt-4o"`
- `"haiku"` (no @) → sdk_model=`"haiku"`, real_model=`"haiku"` (backwards compatible)

A **model rewrite map** is built at startup: `{"sonnet": "litellm/gpt-4o", "haiku": "litellm/gpt-4o-mini"}`. When no `@` is present, no rewrite entry is added.

---

### Section 2: Proxy Model Rewriting

The backend proxy (`proxy.py`) intercepts `POST` requests to paths containing `messages`. It parses the JSON body, finds the `model` field (which the SDK resolves to full names like `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`), and checks if it contains a tier keyword (`haiku`, `sonnet`, `opus`). If a match exists in the rewrite map, the model is replaced with the real model name before forwarding upstream.

The upstream URL becomes configurable via `ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM` env var (defaults to `https://api.anthropic.com`). This lets you point to LiteLLM, Bifrost, or any other proxy.

**Limitation**: If two env vars use the same `@suffix` but different real models, the last one wins. In practice this is fine since different subagents should use different tiers if they need different real models.

---

### Section 3: SDK Routing

Currently in container mode, `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` points to Bifrost directly, bypassing the backend proxy. We change this so the SDK always goes through the backend proxy (which then forwards to the configurable upstream). This ensures model rewriting applies to all SDK calls.

```
SDK (in sidecar) → backend proxy (/anthropic/) → rewrite model → ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM
```

The `/v1/models` endpoint stays as an unauthenticated passthrough so the SDK can still resolve short aliases (`haiku` → `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`).

---

### Section 4: Subagent Model Passing

Restore `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL` and `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL` in `config.py`. The SDK alias (parsed from `@suffix`) is passed as `AgentDefinition.model`. The `"inherit"` fallback remains available when no subagent model is configured.

---

**Summary of file changes**:
- `config.py`: Add parsing helper, restore subagent model vars, build rewrite map
- `proxy.py`: Add model rewriting on POST requests, configurable `ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM`
- `agent.py`: Route `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` through backend proxy, pass parsed SDK models to subagent definitions
- `.env` / `.env.example`: Document the `@suffix` format

Does this design look right? Any section you want to revise?

> DEVELOPER

     1→import asyncio
     2→import logging
     3→from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     4→from pathlib import Path
     5→
     6→from fastapi import FastAPI
     7→from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
     8→from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
     9→from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
    10→
    11→from app.routes import tables, query, chat, langfuse_status, config, session
    12→from app.container_manager import container_manager
    13→from app.mcp_sse import mcp_app
    14→from app.config import CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→from app.session_manager import session_manager
    19→
    20→
    21→async def _cleanup_loop():
    22→    while True:
    23→        await asyncio.sleep(60)
    24→        try:
    25→            removed = session_manager.cleanup_stale(ttl_seconds=300)
    26→            if removed:
    27→                logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d stale sessions", removed)
    28→            if container_manager is not None:
    29→                container_removed = container_manager.cleanup_expired()
    30→                if container_removed:
    31→                    logger.info("Background cleanup: removed %d expired containers", container_removed)
    32→        except Exception:
    33→            logger.exception("Error in background cleanup loop")
    34→
    35→
    36→@asynccontextmanager
    37→async def lifespan(app):
    38→    # Clean up orphaned sidecar containers from a previous unclean shutdown.
    39→    if container_manager is not None:
    40→        orphans = container_manager._cleanup_by_label()
    41→        if orphans:
    42→            logger.info("Startup: cleaned up %d orphaned sidecar containers", orphans)
    43→    task = asyncio.create_task(_cleanup_loop())
    44→    yield
    45→    task.cancel()
    46→    if container_manager is not None:
    47→        container_manager.shutdown_all()
    48→
    49→
    50→app = FastAPI(title="DuckDB Data Agent API", lifespan=lifespan)
    51→
    52→app.add_middleware(
    53→    CORSMiddleware,
    54→    allow_origins=CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS,
    55→    allow_credentials=True,
    56→    allow_methods=["*"],
    57→    allow_headers=["*"],
    58→)
    59→
    60→app.include_router(tables.router)
    61→app.include_router(query.router)
    62→app.include_router(chat.router)
    63→app.include_router(langfuse_status.router)
    64→app.include_router(config.router)
    65→app.include_router(session.router)
    66→
    67→
    68→app.mount("/mcp", mcp_app)
    69→
    70→
    71→@app.get("/api/health")
    72→async def health():
    73→    return {"status": "ok"}
    74→
    75→
    76→# Serve frontend static files in production
    77→STATIC_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "static"
    78→
    79→if STATIC_DIR.is_dir():
    80→    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=STATIC_DIR / "assets"), name="static-assets")
    81→
    82→    @app.get("/{full_path:path}")
    83→    async def serve_frontend(full_path: str):
    84→        """Serve frontend for any non-API route (SPA fallback)."""
    85→        file_path = (STATIC_DIR / full_path).resolve()
    86→        # Prevent path traversal: ensure resolved path is inside STATIC_DIR
    87→        if full_path and file_path.is_file() and file_path.is_relative_to(STATIC_DIR.resolve()):
    88→            return FileResponse(file_path)
    89→        return FileResponse(STATIC_DIR / "index.html")
    90→
